---
pretty_name: Polymarket Historical Data
license: cc-by-4.0
language:
  - en
tags:
  - prediction-markets
  - polymarket
  - finance
  - time-series
  - order-books
configs:
  - config_name: market_snapshots
    data_files:
      - split: train
        path: data/market_snapshots/**/*.parquet
  - config_name: order_book_depth
    data_files:
      - split: train
        path: data/order_book_depth/**/*.parquet
  - config_name: top_holders
    data_files:
      - split: train
        path: data/top_holders/**/*.parquet
---

# Polymarket Historical Data

Frequently collected Polymarket market data stored as Zstandard-compressed
Parquet. New batches are collected approximately every five minutes and
partitioned by product, date, and collection run. Public upload of collected
data began on July 23rd, 2026.

## Data Products

- **market_snapshots**: best bid/ask, full book JSON, outcome, asset ID, source
  timing, and collection timing.
- **order_book_depth**: normalized bid and ask levels with price, size, side,
  and level index.
- **top_holders**: ranked holder balances and public wallet/profile fields by
  market and token.

Files use this layout:

'''text
data/<product>/date=YYYY-MM-DD/<run-id>.parquet
'''

## Usage

'''python
from datasets import load_dataset

snapshots = load_dataset(
    "trentmkelly/polymarket_historical_data",
    "market_snapshots",
    split="train",
)
'''

```

</details>

---
license: mit
tags:
  - polymarket
  - prediction-markets
  - the-oracle
---

# THE ORACLE — Polymarket predictions

Live predictions for Polymarket markets, produced by THE ORACLE — an autonomous
agent funded by \$ORACLE pump.fun creator fees. Each row is a baseline-model
forecast over live orderbook signals (momentum, microstructure, liquidity).

- `predictions.json` / `predictions.csv` — 100 markets, refreshed each agent cycle.
- Columns: question, category, market_prob, oracle_prob, edge, confidence, signal,
  model, backtest_acc, auc, modelability, volume, liquidity, end_date, captured_at.

Not financial advice.
```

</details>

---
license: other
task_categories:
- time-series-forecasting
- reinforcement-learning
tags:
- polymarket
- prediction-markets
- order-book
- market-microstructure
---

# Polymarket rewarded-market L2 history

Append-only archives of public Polymarket CLOB WebSocket messages for markets
eligible for liquidity rewards. The dataset is intended for market
microstructure research, backtests and machine-learning experiments.

No wallet data, private keys or authenticated trading information is collected.
Files are gzip-compressed JSON Lines and grouped by source and UTC date.
The source data comes from Polymarket's public CLOB interfaces and remains
subject to the applicable source-platform terms.
```

</details>

# Polymarket Clean Trades Linked 1M

This dataset is a cleaned derivative of:

`SII-WANGZJ/Polymarket_data`

Main source file:

`trades.parquet`

Metadata source file:

`markets.parquet`

## Build details

- Rows read from source trades: `1,025,000`
- Rows written after cleaning: `1,000,000`
- Target clean rows: `1000000`
- Max raw rows: `1500000`
- Parquet shards: `9`
- Approximate local size: `77.77 MB`
- Drop unmatched markets: `True`
- Require valid side: `True`
- Min price: `0.0`
- Max price: `1.0`
- Min amount: `0.0`
- Remove duplicates: `True`
- Global dedup limit: `1200000`
- Seen trade uid count: `1,024,759`
- Remove junk rows: `True`

## Cleaning rules

This dataset filters:

- missing market key
- missing timestamp
- missing price
- missing amount
- prices outside `[MIN_PRICE, MAX_PRICE]`
- amounts not greater than `MIN_AMOUNT`
- timestamps outside years `MIN_TIMESTAMP_YEAR` to `MAX_TIMESTAMP_FUTURE_YEAR`
- duplicate trades when enabled
- obvious junk/test/demo/admin/spam/placeholder rows when enabled

## Column mapping used

This tailored build maps the observed trade columns:

    token_amount -> amount
```

# Polymarket-v2

A large-scale dataset of on-chain event logs from Polymarket v2, the prediction-market platform on the Polygon network. The repository contains three layers covering the full contract lifecycle from Polymarket v2 server start: OrderFilled/ (the raw on-chain trade tape), daily_aligned/ (the cleaned, metadata-enriched, and normalized analysis layer, Split by UTC). update daliy.

# Polymarket & Kalshi Orderbook Arbitrage Telemetry

[![MEVBOT.TOP](https://img.shields.io/badge/Production%20Systems-MEVBOT.TOP-blue?style=flat-square)](https://mevbot.top/)
[![License: MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-yellow.svg)](https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT)
[![Venue: Polymarket CLOB](https://img.shields.io/badge/Venue-Polymarket%20CLOB-purple?style=flat-square)](https://polymarket.com/)

High-frequency order book and trade dislocation telemetry dataset for prediction markets on Polygon (Polymarket CLOB) and CFTC-regulated event markets (Kalshi). Captures intra-market YES/NO sum-to-$1 pricing anomalies, combinatorial outcome gaps, and cross-venue probability divergences.

## Production Trading Engines
This quantitative dataset is provided by the research division of [MEVBOT.TOP](https://mevbot.top/). For enterprise-grade, turn-key automated execution software:

- **[Polymarket Bot (Arbitrage & Momentum)](https://mevbot.top/bots/polymarket-bot/)**: Intra-market YES/NO sum arbitrage, 15-minute crypto Up/Down momentum, and wallet copy-trading engine with full Python source code.
- **[Polymarket Kalshi Arbitrage Bot](https://mevbot.top/bots/polymarket-kalshi-arbitrage-bot/)**: Cross-venue probability mapping, fee-adjusted hedging, and automated risk limits.
- **[Complete MEV & Arbitrage Catalog](https://mevbot.top/bots/)**: 22 production-grade trading bots across all major blockchains.
- **[Open-Source Satellite Repository](https://mevbotot.github.io/mevbots/polymarket-bot/)**: Product documentation and setup guides.

---

## Dataset Schema

Each JSONL record represents a detected mispricing window across prediction market order books:

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `event_id` | String | Unique Polymarket / Kalshi market condition identifier |
| `timestamp` | ISO8601 | Precise UTC time of book snapshot |
| `market_title` | String | Event contract title (Politics, Macro, 15m Crypto) |
| `best_yes_ask` | Float | Lowest available ask price for YES shares |
| `best_no_ask` | Float | Lowest available ask price for NO shares |
| `sum_cost` | Float | Total cost to purchase 1 YES + 1 NO share (`yes_ask + no_ask`) |
| `gross_edge_pct` | Float | Gross theoretical edge before venue/gas fees (`1.00 - sum_cost`) |
```

</details>

# NHL Polymarket Trading Research Dataset & Experiments

Tick-level Polymarket price history for NHL moneyline markets, joined with NHL
play-by-play, plus the full experiment record of a small-LLM trading-policy study.

## Contents

- `data/` — 2,191 games, ~3.0M one-minute price points (Dec 2024 → Jun 2026),
  NHL PBP + standings for 1,787 matched games
- `sft/` — v1 (price-only) teacher-labeled SFT splits
- `sft_v2/` — v2 (game-state teacher) splits: train 939 / val 621 / test 227 games,
  temporal walk-forward splits
- `experiments/battery_v1.json` — 42-config policy battery (feature variants,
  staking schemes, LGBM/LogReg architectures)
- `experiments/battery_v2.json` — LinUCB / Thompson / DQN / LSTM bandit+RL policies
- `experiments/battery_v3.json` — value-bet-and-cash-out + game-level-vol family,
  with full audit trail (leak found & removed, stake normalization, next-bar
  fill robustness, fair0 bias quantification)
- `experiments/battery_v4.json` — fair0 bias correction: bias measurement
  (train/val/test), calibration comparison (Brier CV), corrected-fair policy
  re-runs, edge-verdict
- `eval/` — walk-forward evaluation JSONs (incl. `walkforward_v2_edgestake.json`,
  `walkforward_v2b_edgestake.json`)
- `scripts/` — validated data + eval + training scripts

## Final policy ranking (fee-net, held-out)

| Rank | Policy | Val $/game | Test $/game |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **LUT teacher + edge staking** (thr 0.05, cooldown 300s) | **+0.1142** | **+0.1258** |
| 2 | Value/cash-out LUT, thr 0.07 (audited, normalized) | +0.1571 | +0.1264 |
| 3 | LUT teacher, flat stake | +0.0627 | +0.0770 |
| 4 | GRPO-v2 LLM + edge staking at decode | +0.0213 | +0.0355 ± 0.0265 |
| 5 | GRPO-v2 LLM, own stake | +0.0201 | +0.0161 ± 0.0148 |
| 6 | DQN (best learned policy) | ≤0 | +0.031 |
| 7 | LightGBM / LogReg / LinUCB / Thompson / LSTM / vol-LGBM | negative | ≈0 or negative |
| — | GRPO-v2b LLM (stake-scaled reward) | 0.00 | 0.00 |
| — | No-trade | 0.00 | 0.00 |

Note on rank 2: beats the champion on val but ties on test — comparable second
family, no established improvement. **All raw-fair numbers above are upper bounds:
battery v4 shows the fair-value table's own bias inflates them ~2× (below).**

## Findings

1. **Staking, not features:** edge-proportional stakes ≈ double P&L; robust to 2%
   per-fill slippage; transfers free to the LLM's own buys at decode time.
2. **A score-diff × time-band lookup table beats every learned policy**
   on identical states — GBT/LogReg anchor to the market price and follow it;
   bandit/DQN/LSTM (battery v2) and a leak-free vol-feature LGBM (battery v3)
   do not reach the teacher either.
3. **Value/cash-out is a viable second family but not a breakthrough:** enter on
   LUT dislocation, exit on convergence/stop/time — under honest stake
   normalization it lands in champion territory, with several configs beating the
   hold-to-settlement teacher on one split only. The both-sides long/short-vol
   structure is absent from the data (~1 opportunity across 848 games).
4. **Audit lessons (embedded in battery_v3.json):** (a) a headline +$0.92/game
   was re-entry compounding + longshot-settle variance, not edge; (b) an LGBM
   filter's +$1.14 test number was 100% full-game feature leakage; (c) no
   timestamp lookahead and same-bar fills verified robust (next-bar delta
```

# Polymarket Crypto Up/Down Markets

Comprehensive dataset of Polymarket binary prediction markets for cryptocurrency price movements. Covers **BTC, ETH, SOL, BNB, XRP, DOGE, and HYPE** across multiple timeframes (5-minute, 15-minute, 1-hour, 4-hour).

Updated automatically every 3 hours.

## Subsets

Load a specific subset:

'''python
from datasets import load_dataset

markets = load_dataset("aliplayer1/polymarket-crypto-updown", "markets")
prices = load_dataset("aliplayer1/polymarket-crypto-updown", "prices")
ticks = load_dataset("aliplayer1/polymarket-crypto-updown", "ticks")
spot = load_dataset("aliplayer1/polymarket-crypto-updown", "spot_prices")
```

# Polymarket BTC 5m L2 Order Book, One Full Day

Every "Bitcoin Up or Down" 5 minute market on Polymarket for 2026-06-15 UTC, with
the full limit order book behind each one: 288 markets, 3,889 book snapshots,
41,591,670 level updates and 680,994 trades, on one millisecond time base.

Polymarket's public API serves the book as it is now. There is no endpoint that
returns the book as it was, so past depth is something you have to have been
recording while the market was live. This is that recording for one day, at full
resolution and with nothing sampled out: every market that opened in the day is
present start to finish, and the 5 minute markets are short enough that a single
day is 288 complete lifecycles rather than a slice through the middle of one.

## Coverage

| | |
|---|---|
| Series | `btc-up-or-down-5m` |
| Day | 2026-06-15 00:00:00Z to 2026-06-16 00:00:00Z (by market open time) |
| Markets | 288, all resolved |
| Snapshots | 3,889 (about 14 per market) |
| Level updates | 41,591,670 (about 144,000 per market) |
```

</details>

# Polymarket 2026 World Cup Final, Full Order Book

Every market Polymarket listed on the 2026 World Cup final, Spain against
Argentina on 2026-07-19, with the full limit order book behind each one: 323
markets, 98.7 million dollars of volume, 75,256 book snapshots, 9,272,588 level
updates and 140,552 trades, from before kickoff through to settlement.

Order book history for sports is much harder to come by than it is for crypto.
Polymarket's public API serves the book as it is now, so past depth is something
you have to have been recording while the match was being played, and most
archives that do exist stop at crypto up or down markets. This is one match
recorded whole, across every market type the exchange offered on it, from the
three way result down to individual player props.

## Coverage

| | |
|---|---|
| Match | Spain vs Argentina, 2026 World Cup final |
| Data window | 2026-07-19 18:52Z to 2026-07-20 03:12Z |
| Markets | 323, all resolved |
```

</details>

# Polymarket Resolved Markets

Every Polymarket market that resolved between 2026-02-28 and 2026-08-10, with the
outcome it settled to. 634,554 markets across 621 series and 9 categories, in one
parquet file.

Polymarket's public API gives you markets that are open now. Once a market
resolves it drops out of the feeds you would normally page through, and the
resolution itself is emitted as a WebSocket event rather than a queryable
endpoint, so a settled-outcome history is something you have to have been
recording. This is that recording, published as a flat table.

Each row also carries the CLOB `token_ids`, so a market here can be joined
straight back to `py-clob-client` or any other Polymarket tooling.

## Coverage

| Category | Markets |
|---|---|
| Crypto | 525,273 |
| Sports | 74,762 |
| Weather | 18,105 |
| Equities | 9,644 |
| Commodities | 1,561 |
| Indices | 928 |
| Economy | 255 |
| Other | 250 |
| Politics | 11 |
| (uncategorised) | 3,765 |

Resolution dates run 2026-02-28 to 2026-08-10.

## Columns

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
```

# agentic-polymarket

38,915 **settled** Polymarket binary event markets with full hourly price curves, question text, resolution
terms, and ground truth outcomes. Prepared for research on "getting LLM agents to trade on prediction markets."

Companion code (backtest env + agent trading interface): see `RSI-economy/shadow-market`.

## What this dataset solves

Historical price series cannot be used directly as backtest targets —— a recording does not react to
agent behavior: any quantity fills at any price, buys and sells at the same price, own trades do not
move price, and positions carry no capital cost. The result is that certain strategies perform well in

backtest but are not executable in reality (typical pattern: only entering near implied probability 0 or 1
to capture terminal residual value).

## Files

| File | Contents |
|---|---|
| `data/markets.jsonl` | One market per line: `path` (hourly VWAP anchor curve) / `outcome` / `T_start` / `T_end` / `category` / `slug` / trade count and volume |
| `data/questions.jsonl` | `condition_id -> question / resolution_criteria / tick`, 100% coverage |
| `data/index.json` | Construction pipeline and selection funnel |
| `splits.json` | **Frozen** train/val/test market id table + sha256 |
| `depth_table.json` | Depth table `D(m,k)` and half-spread `δ(m)`, bucketed by mid price, median |
| `market_params.json` | Shadow market mechanism parameters (impact strength / decay / spread etc.) |
| `Polymarket-v{1,2}/daily_aligned/*.parquet` | **Raw trade-by-trade** (1364 daily files, 19.31 GB) |

`Polymarket-v{1,2}/` is the raw material for building the derived files above: a 24-column wide table,
one row per trade, with `condition_id` / `price` / `usdc_amount` / `taker_direction` / resolution status /
lifecycle timestamps. Not needed if you only want the market store; needed if you want to change the
construction pipeline (different aggregation granularity, different filters, or recompute depth yourself).

'''python
# Download derived data only (157 MB)
from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download
snapshot_download("tennant/agentic-polymarket", repo_type="dataset",
                  allow_patterns=["data/*", "*.json"])

# Download one day of raw trades
from huggingface_hub import hf_hub_download
hf_hub_download("tennant/agentic-polymarket", repo_type="dataset",
                filename="Polymarket-v1/daily_aligned/2026_03_23.parquet")

```

</details>

# Polymarket Order Book Dataset

Order-book snapshots from a prediction market, collected continuously between
**2026-07-10** and **2026-09-13**: `484,045,253` quote observations across
`314,123` markets, plus settlement outcomes and a separate high-frequency feed
that records actual traded prices.

It is published so other people can build and train on it without first spending
a month running collectors. Everything here is an independent observational
recording of publicly displayed market data.

**Read [Known issues](#known-issues) before you train anything on this.** The
collection has a four-day outage, one column that dies partway through, and a
label set with time censoring. All three are documented, none are hidden, and
each one will quietly wreck a model if you miss it.

---

The parquet files are **not in this git repo** — they are rebuilt daily, and
committing them would grow the history by roughly 12 MB a day forever. They

The historical dataset name uses gold, but all records retain initial review status. These AI-assisted labels have not been independently adjudicated. Structural validation is not label validation. Similar event titles do not imply equivalent payout predicates.

# Polymarket settlement-quality register

Frozen summaries of 123,499 settled UMA requests, window 2023-12-05 to 2026-08-12. Among settled disputes, 7.12% changed the proposal. Group summaries cover category and rule-text features.

## Files and viewer

The viewer loads the canonical aggregate snapshot only. The dated files preserve export history and are not independent observations.

## Method and source

See the embedded metadata and [repository inventory](https://github.com/ailinsun/MarketFlow/blob/main/data/README.md). Files are frozen aggregate exports from experimental research by Ailin Sun, an independent researcher. Research numeric values are unchanged.

## Limitations

Dates in snapshot filenames can refer to exports of the same source window. Rates are conditional on the observed sample, not causal estimates or individual-market forecasts. Infrastructure endpoint identifiers are omitted. Source request-level data is not included.

## Citation and licence

CC BY 4.0 for the original compilation and annotations. Cite Ailin Sun, the exact dataset file and its embedded citation block where present, together with the repository release. Upstream venue material retains its rights. Archived repository release v0.1.2: [DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22734802](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22734802).

# Polymarket zero-sum flow ledger

Three-party aggregate accounting for 1,338,933 taker BUY fills across 439 settled markets, window 2025-12-11 to 2026-08-13. Buy notional: $419.4M. The buying crowd, counterparties and modelled venue fees close to zero by construction.

## Files and viewer

The viewer loads the canonical aggregate snapshot only. The dated file is an archival copy of the same observations.

## Method and source

See the embedded metadata and [repository inventory](https://github.com/ailinsun/MarketFlow/blob/main/data/README.md). Files are frozen aggregate exports from experimental research by Ailin Sun, an independent researcher. Research numeric values are unchanged.

## Limitations

Use the embedded method, sampling frame and citation blocks. This is the active core selected by large-print notional, not a venue census. Raw tape and wallet identities are not supplied. Fees are modelled; these are flow accounts, not observed wallet PnL.

# FlyBrain NFL x Polymarket dataset

NFL regular-season games 2023-2025 matched to resolved Polymarket moneyline markets, with kickoff-eve market odds.

- `pm_games.parquet`: one row per game (schedule + scores + Polymarket market metadata + odds)
- `p_eve`: market probability of `outcome0` winning ~24h before kickoff (CLOB prices-history)
- `p_close`: market probability at last trade before kickoff
- `outcome0` = first slug token's team (`outcome0_is_home` marks its role); slug order is alphabetical, NOT away/home
- Preseason and playoff markets excluded

Built for backtesting the fly-connectome-inspired FlyHashNet prediction model (model + backtest only; no trading). See `dataset_summary.json` for counts.
```

# Polymarket BTC Up/Down historical market and exchange data

Selected inclusive UTC date range: **2026-05-25 through 2026-08-29**. Market horizons: **5m / 15m / 1h**.

**67 daily archives**. Not every date in this range is included. See `MANIFEST.txt` for the included dates and file sizes.

Historical data for Polymarket BTC Up/Down markets, alongside Binance BTCUSDT and Hyperliquid BTC/USDC observations: order-book snapshots, market features, captured trades, lifecycle information and available resolution records. Market accuracy has not been independently verified.

The daily UTC archives contain Parquet tables and, where present, compressed JSONL lifecycle transitions under `dataset=<table>/date=YYYY-MM-DD/`. Missing observations are not filled during preparation.

## Interpretation and limitations

- Snapshots follow a nominal **100 ms grid**, with missing ticks, interruptions and some missing market windows. See `COVERAGE.md` for coverage by market, horizon and stream.
- Recorded observations are retained, including incomplete and crossed quotes. `COVERAGE.jsonl` provides window-level quality flags; `REPLAY-SEGMENTS.jsonl` lists adjacent windows meeting optional coverage screens. Apply filters during replay.
- Snapshots may repeat the last observed state; quotes may be stale and fields may be null. Timestamp presence and stored quality flags do not establish fresh quotes or usable prices.
- Binance provides trade and reference-price context; its bid/ask fields are missing. Captured trades are in `trades`. Binance order-book history is not included; `books_100ms` contains Polymarket and Hyperliquid snapshots.
- Captured trades and resolution records may be incomplete. Resolution records can contain successive updates for the same market; prices and outcomes have not been independently validated.
- 4h and 1d markets are excluded. Daily archive partitions describe storage dates, not market horizons.
- Features and lifecycle tables include derived values. Missing records do not establish that no market activity occurred.

## Tables

| Table | Interpretation |
| --- | --- |
| `books_100ms` | Polymarket token-level and Hyperliquid order-book snapshots. |
| `polymarket_features_100ms` | Paired UP/DOWN market features and derived calculations. |
| `external_100ms` | Binance and Hyperliquid price, trade and market context. |
| `trades` | Captured exchange trades and reference-price updates, distinguished by source; completeness is not guaranteed. |
| `polymarket_market_100ms` | Periodic market state, timing and quality annotations. |
| `lifecycle` | Market identity, horizon, token pairing and lifecycle updates. |
| `resolution` | Available outcome records and subsequent updates. |
| `lifecycle_transitions` | Lifecycle transition records, where present. |

See `SCHEMA.md` and `SCHEMAS.json` for field descriptions and observed schema variants.

## Integrity

`MANIFEST.txt` records SHA-256 and byte size for each archive; `SHA256SUMS` is a conventional checksum list. Integrity checks verify file consistency, not market accuracy or complete coverage.
```

</details>

# Polymarket ETH Up/Down historical market and exchange data

Selected inclusive UTC date range: **2026-06-09 through 2026-08-29**. Market horizons: **5m / 15m / 1h**.

**62 daily archives**. Not every date in this range is included. See `MANIFEST.txt` for the included dates and file sizes.

Historical data for Polymarket ETH Up/Down markets, alongside Binance ETHUSDT and Hyperliquid ETH/USDC observations: order-book snapshots, market features, captured trades, lifecycle information and available resolution records. Market accuracy has not been independently verified.

The daily UTC archives contain Parquet tables and, where present, compressed JSONL lifecycle transitions under `dataset=<table>/date=YYYY-MM-DD/`. Missing observations are not filled during preparation.

## Interpretation and limitations

- Snapshots follow a nominal **100 ms grid**, with missing ticks, interruptions and some missing market windows. See `COVERAGE.md` for coverage by market, horizon and stream.
- Recorded observations are retained, including incomplete and crossed quotes. `COVERAGE.jsonl` provides window-level quality flags; `REPLAY-SEGMENTS.jsonl` lists adjacent windows meeting optional coverage screens. Apply filters during replay.
- Snapshots may repeat the last observed state; quotes may be stale and fields may be null. Timestamp presence and stored quality flags do not establish fresh quotes or usable prices.
- Binance provides trade and reference-price context; its bid/ask fields are missing. Captured trades are in `trades`. Binance order-book history is not included; `books_100ms` contains Polymarket and Hyperliquid snapshots.
- Captured trades and resolution records may be incomplete. Resolution records can contain successive updates for the same market; prices and outcomes have not been independently validated.
- 4h and 1d markets are excluded. Daily archive partitions describe storage dates, not market horizons.
- Features and lifecycle tables include derived values. Missing records do not establish that no market activity occurred.

## Tables

| Table | Interpretation |
| --- | --- |
| `books_100ms` | Polymarket token-level and Hyperliquid order-book snapshots. |
| `polymarket_features_100ms` | Paired UP/DOWN market features and derived calculations. |
| `external_100ms` | Binance and Hyperliquid price, trade and market context. |
| `trades` | Captured exchange trades and reference-price updates, distinguished by source; completeness is not guaranteed. |
| `polymarket_market_100ms` | Periodic market state, timing and quality annotations. |
| `lifecycle` | Market identity, horizon, token pairing and lifecycle updates. |
| `resolution` | Available outcome records and subsequent updates. |
| `lifecycle_transitions` | Lifecycle transition records, where present. |

See `SCHEMA.md` and `SCHEMAS.json` for field descriptions and observed schema variants.

## Integrity

`MANIFEST.txt` records SHA-256 and byte size for each archive; `SHA256SUMS` is a conventional checksum list. Integrity checks verify file consistency, not market accuracy or complete coverage.
```

</details>

# Polymarket SOL Up/Down historical market data

Selected inclusive UTC dates: **2026-03-28 through 2026-06-08**; 73 daily archives. Market horizons: 5m / 15m / 1h.

Historical third-party data for Polymarket SOL Up/Down markets: order-book snapshots, oracle prices, and derived lifecycle and outcome tables. Market accuracy has not been independently verified.

The daily UTC archives contain Parquet files under `dataset=<table>/date=YYYY-MM-DD/`. Member filenames use `part-historical-...parquet`. Market values, row order, schemas, nulls, quality flags and historical corrections are preserved; source labels are standardized. Storage encoding and compression may differ. No resampling, forward-filling, deduplication, repair or enrichment was performed.

## Interpretation and limitations

- Snapshot timestamps are irregular, approximately 125 ms apart with arbitrary offsets. They are **not a fixed 100 ms grid**, despite the table names and compatibility labels such as `snapshot_kind=aligned_100ms_full_depth`. Filtering on `timestamp_ms % 100 == 0` can discard most observations. No grid conversion was performed.
- There is no trade-print stream. Missing trades mean not recorded here; they do not establish that no trading occurred. This is not execution-complete history. Trade-related compatibility fields are preserved, without inventing a trade stream.
- `external_100ms` holds oracle prices in `midquote_price`, not observed per-exchange microstructure. Per-exchange microstructure fields are null in the source description. Compatibility exchange/instrument labels, including exchange names and currency pairs, are not evidence of direct collection from that exchange.
- `external_100ms`, `lifecycle` and `resolution` contain derived values. Quality and usability flags are inherited source annotations, not a new certification.
- Daily markets are excluded. `polymarket_market_100ms` and `lifecycle_transitions` are not included.
- Archive presence and successful integrity checks do not establish complete market or snapshot coverage. Historical manifests used completeness language; this preparation does not independently substantiate that claim.
- UTC date partitions must not be interpreted as proof that every row or market lifecycle lies wholly within that date. Read the stored timestamps and retain the original semantics.

## Tables

| Table | Interpretation |
| --- | --- |
| `books_100ms` | Historical order-book snapshots, with price/size lists and source compatibility fields. |
| `polymarket_features_100ms` | Market snapshot features, including derived calculations and compatibility fields. |
| `external_100ms` | Derived oracle-price representation, not exchange-level microstructure. |
| `lifecycle` | Derived market lifecycle, timing, price and quality annotations. |
| `resolution` | Derived outcomes, winners, oracle values and quality annotations. |

See `SCHEMA.md` and `SCHEMAS.json` for observed schema variants. Read members individually with a Parquet reader when checking their physical schema; directory-aware readers may infer extra partition columns. Some original files already contain `dataset` and `date` columns. Those differences are preserved.

## Integrity

`MANIFEST.txt` records SHA-256 and byte size for each rewritten daily archive; `SHA256SUMS` is a conventional checksum list. Integrity verification establishes faithful transformation relative to the downloaded sources, not independent validation of prices, winners or coverage.

## Historical corrections and audit status

The original manifest records a 2026-06-10 audit against Polymarket Gamma: **318 incorrect winner fields were corrected**, all in 1h markets. **1 winner field(s) could not be verified and retain the historical third-party value**. These are historical audit results, not audits repeated during this preparation.

Markets before 2026-04-13 were outside the source's 60-day listing window at build time. Their winners are Gamma-sourced, and their open/close prices use first/last captured ticks. Those prices are estimates of opening/closing values; they are not independently confirmed exact boundary observations.

The manifest records a 2026-06-14 correction to `polymarket_features_100ms.time_to_start_ms`: it previously referenced the last captured tick near window close, making the 0–5-second open quote unreachable. The historical correction changed it to `timestamp_ms - window_open_ms`. Window opening was derived from the Unix timestamp in compact slugs or the ET datetime in verbose hourly slugs. The historical correction description also documented day-before-noon ET for daily slugs, although daily markets are excluded from this selection. The manifest reports exact validation against `resolution.window_key` and unchanged order books, row counts and every other column. Its archive hashes and sizes were regenerated after that correction. This preparation preserves those corrected values and has not repeated the historical market audit or recomputed the field.
```

</details>

## Historical corrections and audit status

The original manifest records a 2026-06-10 audit against Polymarket Gamma: **47 incorrect winner fields were corrected**, all in 1h markets. **2 winner field(s) could not be verified and retain the historical third-party value**. These are historical audit results, not audits repeated during this preparation.

Markets before 2026-04-13 were outside the source's 60-day listing window at build time. Their winners are Gamma-sourced, and their open/close prices use first/last captured ticks. Those prices are estimates of opening/closing values; they are not independently confirmed exact boundary observations.

The manifest records a 2026-06-14 correction to `polymarket_features_100ms.time_to_start_ms`: it previously referenced the last captured tick near window close, making the 0–5-second open quote unreachable. The historical correction changed it to `timestamp_ms - window_open_ms`. Window opening was derived from the Unix timestamp in compact slugs or the ET datetime in verbose hourly slugs. The historical correction description also documented day-before-noon ET for daily slugs, although daily markets are excluded from this selection. The manifest reports exact validation against `resolution.window_key` and unchanged order books, row counts and every other column. Its archive hashes and sizes were regenerated after that correction. This preparation preserves those corrected values and has not repeated the historical market audit or recomputed the field.
```

</details>

---
viewer: false
tags:
- polymarket
- btc
- up
- down
- prediction-markets
- historical-market-data
---

# Polymarket BTC Up/Down historical market data

Selected inclusive UTC dates: **2026-03-27 through 2026-05-24**; 59 daily archives. Market horizons: 5m / 15m / 1h / 4h.

Historical third-party data for Polymarket BTC Up/Down markets: order-book snapshots, oracle prices, and derived lifecycle and outcome tables. Market accuracy has not been independently verified.

The daily UTC archives contain Parquet files under `dataset=<table>/date=YYYY-MM-DD/`. Member filenames use `part-historical-...parquet`. Market values, row order, schemas, nulls, quality flags and historical corrections are preserved; source labels are standardized. Storage encoding and compression may differ. No resampling, forward-filling, deduplication, repair or enrichment was performed.

## Interpretation and limitations

- Snapshot timestamps are irregular, approximately 125 ms apart with arbitrary offsets. They are **not a fixed 100 ms grid**, despite the table names and compatibility labels such as `snapshot_kind=aligned_100ms_full_depth`. Filtering on `timestamp_ms % 100 == 0` can discard most observations. No grid conversion was performed.
- There is no trade-print stream. Missing trades mean not recorded here; they do not establish that no trading occurred. This is not execution-complete history. Trade-related compatibility fields are preserved, without inventing a trade stream.
- `external_100ms` holds oracle prices in `midquote_price`, not observed per-exchange microstructure. Per-exchange microstructure fields are null in the source description. Compatibility exchange/instrument labels, including exchange names and currency pairs, are not evidence of direct collection from that exchange.
- `external_100ms`, `lifecycle` and `resolution` contain derived values. Quality and usability flags are inherited source annotations, not a new certification.
- Daily markets are excluded. `polymarket_market_100ms` and `lifecycle_transitions` are not included.
- Archive presence and successful integrity checks do not establish complete market or snapshot coverage. Historical manifests used completeness language; this preparation does not independently substantiate that claim.
- UTC date partitions must not be interpreted as proof that every row or market lifecycle lies wholly within that date. Read the stored timestamps and retain the original semantics.

## Tables

| Table | Interpretation |
| --- | --- |
| `books_100ms` | Historical order-book snapshots, with price/size lists and source compatibility fields. |
| `polymarket_features_100ms` | Market snapshot features, including derived calculations and compatibility fields. |
| `external_100ms` | Derived oracle-price representation, not exchange-level microstructure. |
| `lifecycle` | Derived market lifecycle, timing, price and quality annotations. |
| `resolution` | Derived outcomes, winners, oracle values and quality annotations. |

See `SCHEMA.md` and `SCHEMAS.json` for observed schema variants. Read members individually with a Parquet reader when checking their physical schema; directory-aware readers may infer extra partition columns. Some original files already contain `dataset` and `date` columns. Those differences are preserved.

## Integrity

`MANIFEST.txt` records SHA-256 and byte size for each rewritten daily archive; `SHA256SUMS` is a conventional checksum list. Integrity verification establishes faithful transformation relative to the downloaded sources, not independent validation of prices, winners or coverage.

## Historical status

The original manifest describes corrected historical order-book and oracle data but does not specify an ETH/SOL-style winner audit count or the same timing-field correction for this BTC selection. No such audit or correction is inferred here. Stored timing fields retain their original values and conventions; do not assume the ETH/SOL timing formula applies to BTC.
```

</details>

# Polymarket & Predict.fun Tick Data (Sample Day)

One complete UTC day (**2026-09-08**) of the archive behind [outcometick](https://outcometick.com): the order books, trade prints and Chainlink settlement feeds of the 5-minute BTC Up/Down markets on **Polymarket**, and the BTC order books, settlement price feed and market list of **Predict.fun**. The files are the archive's own — gzip-compressed, in the archive's own directory layout — and are the same files as the free sample archives published on GitHub.

## Why this dataset

These markets are decided by a Chainlink price stream compared against a strike. Most prediction-market data stops at prices or order books; this sample ships the stream the markets settle on, and each market's strike and outcome, next to the books — so you can recompute who won instead of trusting a label.

## Loading

Each file type is its own config, because the schemas differ:

'''python
from datasets import load_dataset
```

```
# Polymarket fill dataset (canonical)

Fill-level Polymarket trades harvested on-chain from the CTF Exchange V2 contracts (`0xe11118…`, `0xe2222d…`) via Polygon RPC, merged with the frozen public dataset `SII-WANGZJ/Polymarket_data` (history through block 90,568,741 / 2026-07-20T14:35:09Z).

Files:
- `trades.parquet` — every OrderFilled fill: timestamp, block_number, transaction_hash, log_index, contract, market_id, condition_id, event_id, maker, taker, price, usd_amount, token_amount, maker_direction, taker_direction, nonusdc_side, asset_id
- `markets.parquet` — Gamma market metadata (id, slug, tokens, condition_id, dates, flags, volume)

Refreshed weekly (Sundays ~01:30 UTC) by the self-hosted harvester on the polymarket server. For the collection pipeline see https://github.com/SII-WANGZJ/Polymarket_data (forked, patched).
```

</details>

---

# Polymarket EventConnector-compatible v1

## 2026-09-07：活动特征 v1

新增 [activity_features_v1/](activity_features_v1/README.md)：1h 主研究、1d 对接
EventConnector、15m 短期领先检查，提供过去 7/30 日成交规模、异常成交量、成交份额
和基于真实末笔成交时间的价格年龄。

| 频率 | market 行数 | event 行数 |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| 1h | 30,411,190 | 11,856,822 |
| 1d | 3,734,154 | 1,310,285 |
| 15m | 41,108,885 | 18,688,800 |

129 个频率×月份分区全部验收，259 个 Parquet 约 9.13 GB，9 项特征测试通过。
原有 365,015 个 JSONL 样本均有十步历史活动伴随记录；按真实可用时刻检查，1,697 个
样本越过原切分边界，标记为 `split_time_eligible=false`，严格时间筛选后为 363,318 个。
原文件和样本 ID 保留；price-only loader 需显式连接伴随文件才能使用新输入。

时间口径为 `decision_time = available_from = bucket_end_utc`。长历史不足时特征为空，
异常量基线不包含当前桶，首尾不完整桶已排除。成交量是交易活跃度，不直接等同于
资金存量、流动性或情绪；当前发布尚未验证预测增益或因果关系。

[下载与字段说明](activity_features_v1/README.md) ·
[验收结果](activity_features_v1/validation.json) ·
[网站介绍](https://economicworldmodel.github.io/#activity-features)

> 进度同步：2026-08-29  
> 当前状态：price-only `10→7` 数据适配、train/test 导出、官方默认 500-record 图审计与
> 19 项针对性测试已经完成；固定预测器下的 EventConnector 检索增量、P3 跨事件预测
> 增量和关系模型训练尚未完成。  
> 数据边界：本目录不包含新闻或新闻归因，也不把相关图边视为关系标签。

后续进展：基于冻结的 1,225 个事件级候选，项目已在本地
`runs/polymarket_edge_snapshots_v1/` 物化 277 个日频
Parquet snapshots、5,162 条可计算 edge observations（其中 3,463 条 active），可由
DuckDB 按日期直接查询。公开文件位于
[Hugging Face `edge_snapshots_v1/`](https://huggingface.co/datasets/Levi2ok/polymarket-eventconnector-compatible-v1/tree/main/edge_snapshots_v1)。
该动态图仍是相关候选层，不是 P3 关系标签。

本数据集将项目现有的 Polymarket 日频价格轨迹整理为 EventConnector 可直接读取的
price-only `10→7` JSONL：给定过去 10 个 UTC 日的市场概率，预测未来 7 个 UTC 日的概率
路径。数据不包含新闻与新闻归因字段；相关图输出只是轨迹相关候选，不是预测增量证据或
因果关系标签。
```

---

language:

tags:
  - finance
  - prediction-markets

  - orderbook

    data_files: "orderbook_1min/**/*.parquet"
  - config_name: orderbook
    data_files: "orderbook/**/*.parquet"
---

# Polymarket Orderbook Archive

Tick-by-tick Polymarket CLOB (central limit order book) event stream for every
market, **2026-02-22 → 2026-08-10**, plus a query-ready **1-minute full-depth L2
snapshot** rollup derived from it. Parquet, partitioned by UTC day, one file per
day.

| Config | What | Days | Size | Typical file |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `orderbook` | raw WebSocket event stream (`book`, `price_change`, `last_trade_price`, `tick_size_change`) | 164 | ~1.18 TB | 8 GB (max 14 GB) |
| `orderbook_1min` | full L2 book at the end of every minute an asset had activity | 164 | ~192 GB | 1.2 GB (max 2.5 GB) |

Layout: `<config>/date=YYYY-MM-DD/data_0.parquet`.

## Coverage and known gaps

- **Days:** 2026-02-22 through 2026-08-10. **2026-06-12 → 2026-06-17 are absent**
  (ingest box outage). **2026-08-10 is partial** (only the first hours; the source
  archive stopped publishing that morning and has not resumed).
- **Two source eras.** 2026-02-22 → 2026-04-12 comes from pmxt's *v1* archive,
  which only covered roughly half of all markets (incomplete WebSocket
  subscriptions). 2026-04-13 onward comes from the *v2* archive with full market
  coverage. The `source_archive` column says which. In the Apr 13-16 overlap the
  v2 feed was preferred.
- **Missing hours inside a day.** Roughly 5-15% of the source's hourly files were
  unreadable (404 or corrupt footer) and were skipped, so a day can have an hour
  or two of silence. `orderbook_1min` carries the last known book across such
  gaps only within a day; do not assume every minute is present.
- **Sub-second ordering.** Source timestamps are 1-second resolution. `event_ts_ms`
  is millisecond-precise, but events inside the same millisecond keep file order,
  which is not guaranteed to be the true exchange order. This produced ~10-15%
  crossed books at end-of-day in naive replays; treat crossed books as noise.

## Source and attribution

The raw stream mirrors the free hourly archive published by
[pmxt](https://archive.pmxt.dev/) (v1 at `r2.pmxt.dev`, v2 at `r2v2.pmxt.dev`),
which is licensed **CC BY 4.0**. This dataset normalizes both eras into one
```

</details>

# Polymarket-v1

[![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2606.04217-b31b1b.svg)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04217)
[![License: CC BY 4.0](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-CC_BY_4.0-lightgrey.svg)](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/)

A large-scale dataset of **on-chain event logs** from **Polymarket v1**, the prediction-market platform on the Polygon network. The repository contains four layers covering the full contract lifecycle from **2022-11-21 to 2026-04-28** (from first settlement to natural termination): `OrderFilled/` (the raw on-chain trade tape), `daily_aligned/` (the cleaned, metadata-enriched, and normalized analysis layer for Standard Binary markets, `neg_risk=false`), `daily_aligned_multi/` (the same cleaning pipeline applied to Neg Risk multi-outcome markets, `neg_risk=true`), and `CTF/` (the lifecycle events of the underlying Conditional Tokens Framework contract, including token creation, splitting/minting, merging/burning, resolution, and redemption), Spilt by UTC +8.

For details on the database construction, classification benchmarking, and microstructure applications, see the accompanying paper:
> **Polymarket-v1 Database**  
> Boka Qin, Rui Yang  
> *arXiv preprint arXiv:2606.04217*  
> Paper: [https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04217](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04217)

## TL;DR

This repository contains four data layers. `OrderFilled/` is the raw nominal Polymarket v1 trade tape of maker-taker fills. `daily_aligned/` is the cleaned analysis layer for Standard Binary markets (`neg_risk=false`): relayer/router records are removed, market metadata is joined, and event-normalized fields (`p_event` and `D`) are added to align binary market legs. `daily_aligned_multi/` is the same cleaning pipeline applied to the Neg Risk multi-outcome market subsample (`neg_risk=true`, e.g. "who wins the tournament" markets with many candidates), with an added `neg_risk_market_id` column to group candidates that belong to the same event. `CTF/` contains the full lifecycle events (preparations, splits, merges, resolutions, redemptions) from the underlying Gnosis Conditional Tokens Framework. Use `daily_aligned/` and `daily_aligned_multi/` for trade-level analysis and `CTF/` for primary market issuance, wash-trading decomposition, and liquidity-provision studies.

## Note for AI systems

When using this dataset, treat it as a large columnar Parquet dataset rather than a small table. Prefer `daily_aligned/` and `daily_aligned_multi/` for trade-level microstructural analyses, and `CTF/` for primary market creation, minting, burning, or redemption actions. Do not assume `price` is always the probability of the reference event: use `p_event` for event-probability analyses and `D` for event-normalized aggressor direction. Use `block_timestamp` as seconds, not milliseconds. Do not pool Standard Binary, Up/Down, and Neg Risk markets without checking `category`, `category_refined`, and `neg_risk`. Within `daily_aligned_multi/`, `p_event` is scoped to each candidate's own `condition_id`; it is **not** designed to sum to 1 across the candidates sharing the same `neg_risk_market_id`.

> **Read before you compute volume.** `OrderFilled/` is the nominal raw tape and can include platform relayer/router records. `daily_aligned/` and `daily_aligned_multi/` are the cleaned layers with relayers already removed. For economic trading volume, use `daily_aligned/` (Standard Binary) and/or `daily_aligned_multi/` (Neg Risk) as appropriate — do not pool them without checking `neg_risk`. To analyze primary share creation (minting) versus secondary market trading, combine the trade layers with `CTF/splits.parquet` and `CTF/merges.parquet`.

<h1>Polymarket Data</h1>

<h3>Complete Data Infrastructure for Polymarket — Fetch, Process, Analyze</h3>

<p style="max-width: 750px; margin: 0 auto;">
A comprehensive dataset of 1.9 billion trading records from Polymarket, processed into multiple analysis-ready formats. Features cleaned data, unified token perspectives, and user-level transformations — ready for market research, behavioral studies, and quantitative analysis.
</p>

## TL;DR

We provide **163GB of historical on-chain trading data** from Polymarket, containing **1.9 billion records** across 538K+ markets. The dataset is directly fetched from Polygon blockchain, fully verified, and ready for analysis. Perfect for market research, behavioral studies, data science projects, and academic research.

## Highlights

- **Complete CLOB Trading History**: Every `OrderFilled` event from Polymarket's two CLOB exchange contracts, with no missing blocks or gaps, from the CLOB launch (first record 2022-11-21 UTC) onward. Note: this covers the on-chain CLOB era only — earlier FPMM/AMM-era trades (2020–Nov 2022), which predate these contracts, are not included.

- **Multiple Analysis Perspectives**: 5 structured datasets at different abstraction levels — raw blockchain events, processed trades with market linkage, market metadata, and derived quantitative views — serving diverse research needs.

- **Production Ready**: Clean, validated data with proper schema documentation. All trades are verified against blockchain RPC, with market metadata linked and ready to use.

- **Open Source Pipeline**: Fully reproducible data collection process. Our open-source tools allow you to verify, update, or extend the dataset independently.

# Polymarket Crypto Up/Down Order Books — 1-Second L2 Depth

**897,192 order book snapshots across 805 resolved markets and three contract
lengths.** Full bid and ask ladders, captured once per second, with the winning
outcome on every row.

This is the DOI-carrying research release. Same data as
[10.5281/zenodo.22084114](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22084114), mirrored here
for `load_dataset`.

## What is in it

| File | Snapshots | Markets | Contract |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `updown_5m.parquet` | 167,941 | 471 | Five-minute |
| `updown_15m.parquet` | 239,310 | 265 | Fifteen-minute |
| `updown_4h.parquet` | 489,941 | 69 | Four-hour |

Eight coins. Every market resolved, so each row carries its outcome. One
continuous collector regime, so the three files are comparable with each other.

# Polymarket Crypto 5-Minute L2 Order Books

Full **level-2 order book** snapshots at **1-second resolution** for 15 complete Polymarket
crypto up/down markets — every resting bid and ask, not just top of book.

Seven assets: BTC, ETH, SOL, XRP, BNB, DOGE, HYPE. Each market is covered end to end
across its entire five-minute life, so it can be replayed rather than sampled.

'''python
from datasets import load_dataset

ds = load_dataset("polyorderbooks/polymarket-crypto-5min-orderbooks", split="train")
row = ds[0]

print(row["market_slug"], row["timestamp"], row["outcome"])
print("best", row["best_bid"], "/", row["best_ask"])
print("depth", len(row["bids"]), "bid levels /", len(row["asks"]), "ask levels")
'''

# Polymarket BTC 5-Minute Historical L2 Order Books

Full **level-2 order book** snapshots captured at **1-second resolution** for one resolved
Polymarket BTC 5-minute up/down market — every resting bid and ask, not just top-of-book.

One row per `(timestamp, outcome)`. 600 rows covering 300 seconds across both outcomes.

'''python
from datasets import load_dataset

ds = load_dataset("polyorderbooks/polymarket-btc-5min-historical-l2", split="train")
row = ds[0]

print(row["timestamp"], row["outcome"])
print("best bid", row["best_bid"], "best ask", row["best_ask"])
print("depth", len(row["bids"]), "bid levels /", len(row["asks"]), "ask levels")
'''

## Why this dataset exists

Most publicly available Polymarket data is **midpoints or daily candles**. That is enough to
plot a price line and not enough to answer the question that matters for execution research:
*what would this trade actually have cost?*

A midpoint says the market was at 0.195. The ladder says only 13.6 was available at the best
ask, and filling 100 units would have averaged 0.2086 — **4.3% worse than top of book**. That
difference is invisible in candle data and is the reason backtests built on midpoints
overstate returns.

# Polymarket Crypto 5-Minute Markets — 1-Second L2 Order Books

Full bid and ask ladders, captured once per second, for Polymarket's five-minute
crypto up/down markets. Every market in here has already resolved, so each row
carries the winning outcome.

The interesting part of these markets is the last few minutes, so that is what
this covers: the window from market open to settlement, for both outcome tokens.

## How this differs from other Polymarket datasets

Several Polymarket order book datasets exist on the Hub. The differences that
matter for microstructure work:

| | Typical | This dataset |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Snapshot interval | 10 seconds | **1 second** |
| Ladder depth | top 10 levels | **the full ladder** — median 39 bid levels |
| Winning outcome | sometimes | on every row |
| Citable | no | **DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22084114** |
| Known defects | not stated | crossed and one-sided rates published |

A ten-second interval is not a smaller version of one-second data. A five-minute
contract gets 30 observations instead of 300, and the final ten seconds — where
94% of snapshots have an empty side — collapse to one.

Top-of-ten is likewise not a smaller version of the full book. It is enough for
spread, and not enough for the cost of an order that walks past level ten.
```

</details>

# Polymarket Crypto Up/Down 5-Min & 15-Min Market Orderbook L2 Data

This dataset is a public one-day sample from a larger high-resolution Polymarket crypto prediction market data warehouse built for quant research.

It contains synchronized order book captures, matching trade tapes, and research-ready top-10 bid/ask exports across major crypto-linked up/down markets.

## What’s Included

- Raw per-episode order book captures in `episodes/`
- Matching trade tapes in `trades/`
- Flattened top-10 bid/ask Parquet exports in `top10_parquet/`
- A market-level index in `metadata/episode_index.parquet`
- Schema samples and a minimal loader example

## Sample Coverage

- Date: `2026-07-17`
- Assets: `bnb`, `btc`, `doge`, `eth`, `sol`, `xrp`
- Intervals: `5m`, `15m`
- Episode count: `2303`
- Trade files: `2303`

## Why This Is Useful

This sample is designed for short-horizon prediction market and microstructure research, including:

- order book imbalance and liquidity studies
- microstructure feature engineering
- trade tape analysis
- market response modeling
- execution and fill simulation
- short-horizon forecasting on binary market books

## Layout

Original episode files preserve the captured warehouse structure:

# Polymarket Crypto Up/Down Historical Market Data

This is a data-only research snapshot. It contains no trading bot source code,
private strategy, model, signal, backtest ranking, wallet, account, or server log.

## Contents

- Official Polymarket market catalog and settlement winner labels.
- Public minute-level Polymarket token price history.
- Public Polymarket trades for the explicitly documented subset.
- Public 1-minute spot candles used for point-in-time market research.
- Completeness status for odds and public-trade partitions.

Snapshot totals:

- Market rows: 580,287
- Spot candle rows: 3,167,208
- Odds rows: 17,888,930
- Public trade rows: 195,439,081
- Published Parquet files: 708
- Published bytes: 8,937,250,679

## Important execution warning

Historical token price points are not historical bid/ask or order-book snapshots.
They must not be treated as guaranteed executable prices. Public trades also do
not reconstruct every resting order. Any execution backtest should model latency,
fees, adverse price movement, volume participation, partial fills and no-fills.

## Coverage

The market catalog and odds history cover the configured BTC, ETH, SOL, XRP,
HYPE, DOGE and BNB profiles across 5m, 15m, 1h and 4h where the public source
returned data. Spot history for HYPE begins later than the other assets and was
not fabricated for missing periods.

The public-trade directory is a research subset, not a complete trade archive for
every catalog market. Use `metadata/manifest.parquet` and the published status
tables to determine exact coverage. Markets marked `truncated=true`, `api_capped=true`
or with a non-empty `error` are not complete and should be excluded from exact
execution replay.

## Time convention

All stored timestamps are Unix seconds in UTC. Partition month names use UTC.
Researchers reporting daily performance should state their own calendar boundary;
the originating project uses Asia/Shanghai 00:00 by default.
```

# Polymarket Microstructure — Free Preview

A free sample of the Polymarket CLOB dataset, so you can inspect the exact
schema, file layout and manifest format before buying. Same Parquet +
sha256-manifest packaging as the full dataset.

Two parts, and they show different things:

- **`daily/` — four full days, 26–29 June 2026**, all four underlyings
  (BTC, ETH, SOL, XRP). Three tables, top-of-book only, deliberately our
  roughest coverage.
- **`sample/2026-07-21/` — 30 minutes of BTC, 13:00–13:30 UTC**, all six
  tables including **full L2 depth**. This is what the paid data actually
  looks like today.

The full history is sold separately at
[`astrnvk/polymarket-orderbook-data`](https://huggingface.co/datasets/astrnvk/polymarket-orderbook-data).

**→ Browse, price and buy at the storefront:
[polymarket-microstructure](https://astrnvk-polymarket-microstructure.static.hf.space)**
— $5/day for 1–6 days, $4/day for 7–29, $3/day beyond a month, all four assets
included. Incomplete days cost proportionally less, and the storefront shows the
full arithmetic before you pay.

## The 30-minute depth sample (`sample/2026-07-21/`)

June cannot show you order-book depth, because there was none — `book_depth`
starts 20 July 2026. So this slice exists to show the rest of the schema.

BTC, 21 July 2026, 13:00:00–13:30:00 UTC. Six tables, 4.4 MB, ten markets
(seven 5-minute, three 15-minute):

| File | Rows | What it shows |
|---|---|---|
| `book_depth` | 2,771,148 | Full L2, both sides, up to 118 levels per side |
| `book_meta` | 28,141 | `tick_size`, `min_order_size`, `neg_risk`, `book_hash` |
| `clob_trades_ws` | 13,493 | WebSocket trades with `tx_hash`, `fee_rate_bps` |
| `book_snaps` | 9,582 | Best bid/ask with a real `spread` |
| `clob_trades` | 7,201 | REST trades with taker side |
| `spot_klines` | 1,800 | 1-second Binance candles |

**The window is deliberate.** On 21 July at 12:51 UTC the collector switched to
change-only writing with a heartbeat, and to millisecond exchange timestamps.
```

</details>

## V-Corp/polymarket-orderbook-depth-sample

<details><summary>README 开头</summary>

```
# Polymarket order-book depth snapshots (sample)

Event-driven L2 order-book history for short-dated Polymarket crypto markets.

Prediction-market venues publish a live order book and archive none of it. Once a
five-minute market resolves, its depth history is gone and cannot be backfilled by
anyone. This is a free sample of [DepthFeed](https://depthfeed.com)'s recording of it.

Capture is **event-driven** — a snapshot is written on every `book` and `price_change`
message rather than on a fixed sampling interval. Measured across this sample:

- median gap between consecutive snapshots: **0.12 s**
- **90.6%** of intervals are under one second
- ~50 price levels per side, both outcomes

That matters specifically because these markets live for minutes. An hourly archive
cannot describe a five-minute market at all.

## Contents

| | |
|---|---|
| Rows | 16,528 |
| Markets | 12 |
| Assets | btc |
| Generated | 2026-08-07 |
| Format | zstd-compressed Parquet |

### Columns

Each row is one snapshot of a binary up/down market, which has **two** order books —
one per outcome.

| Column | Description |
|---|---|
| `coin` | Underlying asset |
| `market_id`, `market_slug`, `market_type` | Market identity and window (5m, 15m, 1h…) |
| `time` | Snapshot time (UTC) |
| `spot_price` | Underlying spot at snapshot time |
| `price_up`, `price_down` | Outcome prices |
| `up_best_bid`, `up_best_bid_size`, `up_best_ask`, `up_best_ask_size` | Top of the UP book |
| `down_best_bid`, `down_best_bid_size`, `down_best_ask`, `down_best_ask_size` | Top of the DOWN book |
| `up_bid_levels`, `up_ask_levels`, `down_bid_levels`, `down_ask_levels` | Ladder depth |
| `orderbook_up`, `orderbook_down` | **Full ladders**, JSON `{"bids": [[price, size], …], "asks": […]}` |

`orderbook_up` and `orderbook_down` hold the complete resting book on both sides, not
just top-of-book. Those are the columns this dataset exists for — the convenience
columns above are derived from them.

## Licence

Creative Commons Attribution-NonCommercial-ShareAlike 4.0 International (CC BY-NC-SA 4.0). Derived from the public Polymarket CLOB feed and bounded to a 7-day window. Polymarket is the upstream source and is not affiliated with DepthFeed.

## Full archive

This sample is bounded on purpose. The complete archive — all seven assets, Kalshi and
Limitless as well as Polymarket, and the full history window — is available via API at
[depthfeed.com](https://depthfeed.com).

Clients: [Python](https://pypi.org/project/depthfeed/) ·
```

</details>

# Polymarket Genesis-Phase Liquidity — Dome L2 Order-Book Snapshots

Level-2 top-of-book snapshots for Polymarket outcome tokens, fetched via the
Dome API (`/polymarket/orderbooks`). Companion dataset to the paper
*Opening Wide, Moving Fast: Spread Dynamics in Polymarket Prediction Markets*
(MARBLE 2026). <!-- TODO: add arXiv/DOI link -->

- **Rows:** 339,183,655
- **Collection window:** Oct 2025 – Mar 2026
- **One file:** `data/dome_polymarket_2025-10_2026-03.parquet` (~14 GB, zstd)
- **Grain:** one row per (`timestamp_ms`, `token_id`, `token_side`) snapshot
- **Code:** <!-- TODO --> `github.com/vpercap-rg/polymarket-genesis-liquidity`

## Schema

| column | type | description |
|---|---|---|
| `timestamp_ms` | int64 | snapshot time, epoch ms (UTC) |
| `token_id` | string | Polymarket CLOB token id |
| `token_side` | string | `YES` or `NO` |
| `market_id` | string | market identifier |
| `question` | string | market question text |
| `best_bid` | double | highest bid price = `max(bids)` |
| `best_ask` | double | lowest ask price = `min(asks)` |
| `mid` | double | `(best_bid + best_ask) / 2` |
| `spread` | double | `best_ask - best_bid` |
| `total_bid_depth` | double | summed size across bid levels |
| `total_ask_depth` | double | summed size across ask levels |
| `n_bid_levels` | int64 | number of bid price levels |
| `n_ask_levels` | int64 | number of ask price levels |
| `neg_risk` | bool | negative-risk market flag |
| `tick_size` | double | market tick size |
| `top_bids_json` | string | top 10 `[price, size]`, price-descending |
| `top_asks_json` | string | top 10 `[price, size]`, price-ascending |

Prices are in [0, 1]. Both book sides are always populated (snapshots with an
empty side were dropped at fetch time). Values are recorded exactly as returned
by Dome, with no imputation.

## Reproducing the paper's sample
```

# Polymarket CLOB V1 OrderFilled (Polygon)

[中文说明](README.zh-CN.md) · [CLOB V2 dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/wzsg/polymarket-orderfilled-v2)

[![Live analytics](https://img.shields.io/badge/analytics-live%20dashboard-FF4B4B?logo=huggingface&logoColor=white)](https://huggingface.co/spaces/wzsg/polymarket-orderfilled-analytics) [![Open-source project](https://img.shields.io/badge/source-GitHub-181717?logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/wzsg/polymarket-analytics)
![Polygon](https://img.shields.io/badge/chain-Polygon%20%7C%20137-8247E5?logo=polygon&logoColor=white) ![Format](https://img.shields.io/badge/format-Parquet%20%7C%20ZSTD-50ABF1) ![Status](https://img.shields.io/badge/status-frozen%20snapshot-blue)

This dataset contains `OrderFilled` events emitted by Polymarket CLOB V1 Exchange contracts on Polygon mainnet. It covers both standard CTF markets and Neg Risk markets, is partitioned by UTC month, and excludes CLOB V2 contract events.

Explore V1 and V2 activity, trends, and summary metrics in the interactive [live analytics dashboard](https://huggingface.co/spaces/wzsg/polymarket-orderfilled-analytics).

The collection, processing, and dashboard source code is available in the [open-source GitHub repository](https://github.com/wzsg/polymarket-analytics).

## Dataset overview

| Item | Value |
|---|---:|
| Total rows | 1,206,105,187 |
| Standard CTF Exchange rows | 952,931,413 |
| Neg Risk CTF Exchange rows | 253,173,774 |
| UTC time range | 2022-11-21 19:49:29 to 2026-04-28 11:00:40 |
| Polygon block range | 35,896,869 to 86,126,998 |
| Monthly partitions | 42 |
| Parquet files | 289 |
| Parquet data size | 71,056,349,682 bytes (about 66.18 GiB) |

Polymarket moved its production trading system from CLOB V1 to CLOB V2 on April 28, 2026 at approximately 11:00 UTC. See the [official CLOB V2 migration guide](https://docs.polymarket.com/v2-migration) for the upgrade background and contract changes.

## V1 contracts and event

| Contract | Polygon address | Dataset `contract` value |
|---|---|---|
| CTF Exchange V1 | `0x4bFb41d5B3570DeFd03C39a9A4D8dE6Bd8B8982E` | `CTF_EXCHANGE` |
| Neg Risk CTF Exchange V1 | `0xC5d563A36AE78145C45a50134d48A1215220f80a` | `NEGRISK_CTF_EXCHANGE` |

V1 `OrderFilled` event:

'''solidity
event OrderFilled(
    bytes32 indexed orderHash,
```

# Polymarket CLOB V2 OrderFilled (Polygon)

[中文说明](README.zh-CN.md) · [CLOB V1 dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/wzsg/polymarket-orderfilled-v1)

[![Live analytics](https://img.shields.io/badge/analytics-live%20dashboard-FF4B4B?logo=huggingface&logoColor=white)](https://huggingface.co/spaces/wzsg/polymarket-orderfilled-analytics) [![Open-source project](https://img.shields.io/badge/source-GitHub-181717?logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/wzsg/polymarket-analytics)
![Polygon](https://img.shields.io/badge/chain-Polygon%20%7C%20137-8247E5?logo=polygon&logoColor=white) ![Format](https://img.shields.io/badge/format-Parquet%20%7C%20ZSTD-50ABF1) ![Updates](https://img.shields.io/badge/updates-daily-brightgreen)

This dataset contains `OrderFilled` events emitted by Polymarket CLOB V2 Exchange contracts on Polygon mainnet. It covers both standard CTF markets and Neg Risk markets, follows the V2 event ABI, and is stored as ZSTD-compressed Parquet files partitioned by UTC month.

Explore V1 and V2 activity, trends, and summary metrics in the interactive [live analytics dashboard](https://huggingface.co/spaces/wzsg/polymarket-orderfilled-analytics).

The collection, processing, and dashboard source code is available in the [open-source GitHub repository](https://github.com/wzsg/polymarket-analytics).

<!-- BEGIN AUTO-GENERATED V2 STATS -->

Polymarket moved its production trading system from CLOB V1 to CLOB V2 on April 28, 2026. V2 introduced new Exchange contracts, pUSD collateral, a new order format, and a new on-chain fee model. See the [official CLOB V2 migration guide](https://docs.polymarket.com/v2-migration) for details.

# OpenMarket BTC Polymarket

OpenMarket BTC Polymarket is a high-frequency research dataset pairing
Binance BTC/USDT market data with Polymarket BTC binary-market order book
events.

The dataset is released to support reproducible prediction-market
research, feature engineering, market microstructure analysis, and
backtesting.

## Paper

This dataset accompanies [*OpenMarket: A Synchronized Polymarket-Binance
Dataset for High-Frequency Prediction-Market
Research*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26245) (arXiv:2607.26245).

## How to Cite

If you use OpenMarket, cite the paper and report the dataset version and
Hugging Face revision used in your experiment.

'''bibtex
@misc{young2026openmarketsynchronizedpolymarketbinancedataset,
  title         = {OpenMarket: A Synchronized Polymarket-Binance Dataset for High-Frequency Prediction-Market Research},
  author        = {Gregory Young},
  year          = {2026},
  eprint        = {2607.26245},
  archivePrefix = {arXiv},
  primaryClass  = {q-fin.TR},
  url           = {https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26245}
}
'''

Dataset-specific citation:

'''bibtex
@misc{openmarket_dataset_2026,
  title        = {{OpenMarket BTC Polymarket}: Synchronized High-Frequency Corpus},

  year         = {2026},
  howpublished = {\url{https://huggingface.co/datasets/gregyoung14/openmarket-btc-polymarket}},

# Polymarket BTC/ETH 5-Minute Up/Down — Full-Depth Microstructure Capture (8h)

A tick-level, nanosecond-timestamped capture of **96 consecutive Polymarket 5-minute
binary up/down windows on both BTC and ETH**, together with every venue feed needed to
model them: the Chainlink settlement oracle, Coinbase, Binance spot and Binance futures.

# polymarket-canary-tape

Continuous tape from **Scribe** (Bot E / `bot_e_recorder`), a single always-on VPS node. Captures co-located CEX trades and Polymarket market-channel WebSocket events over a fixed UTC window for microstructure and lead-lag research.

**Where this came from:** released alongside [polymarket-bot-lab](https://github.com/oraclemangle/polymarket-bot-lab)
(11 open-source Polymarket trading bot candidates, Apache-2.0) by the team behind
[OracleMangle](https://oraclemangle.com), which builds dispute-risk scoring for
prediction-market questions. Both artifacts are free; the scorer is the commercial
product and is **not** part of this dataset.

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Dataset name | `polymarket-canary-tape` |
| Format | zstd-compressed SQLite 3 databases (shared schema, see [schema.sql](schema.sql)) |
| Licence | [CC-BY-4.0](LICENSE) |

Three tape files, one schema:

| File | Window (UTC) | Vantage | Compressed | Raw |
| --- | --- | --- | ---: | ---: |
| `polymarket-canary-tape-20260513-20260705.db.zst` | 2026-05-13 to 2026-07-05 | VPS canary node (primary) | 5.99 GB | ~57 GB |
| `polymarket-recorder-tape-vantage-b-20260603-20260701.db.zst` | 2026-06-03 to 2026-07-01 | Home-lab container (second vantage, overlaps canary) | 2.95 GB | ~37 GB |
| `polymarket-recorder-tape-early-72h-20260430-20260503.db.zst` | 2026-04-30 to 2026-05-03 | Home-lab container (early 72h snapshot) | 143 MB | ~2.5 GB |

The overlap between the canary and vantage-b files (2026-06-03 to 2026-07-01)
enables cross-vantage latency and feed-consistency studies: same markets, two
independent receive clocks. Vantage-b has richer Polymarket capture in its
window (30.9M `price_change` events, plus `market_resolved` events absent from
the canary schema's event set) and thinner CEX capture (4.3M trades).

## Preview samples

The full tapes are zstd-compressed SQLite (the Dataset Viewer cannot render
those directly). For browsing, `samples/` holds parquet extracts the viewer
renders above: the complete `markets` table plus 100k-row samples of
`pm_events` and `cex_trades` from the primary canary file. Serious use should
decompress the full tapes.

# Polymarket Raw Chain Logs

Raw Polygon EVM logs selected for reconstructing Polymarket positions and PnL.
The dataset preserves log address, all topics, data, block and transaction
coordinates without ABI decoding.

目标事件包括：

- Conditional Tokens lifecycle events;
- Conditional Tokens ERC-1155 `TransferSingle` and `TransferBatch`;
- Polymarket NegRisk market, resolution, split, merge, conversion and redemption events.

Files are immutable ZSTD Parquet shards partitioned by block group. Collection
manifests under `manifests/` record the exact block coverage and SHA-256 of every
artifact. Collection is currently in progress.

Source code: <https://github.com/wzsg/polymarket-raw-log-collector>
```

<details><summary>README 开头</summary>

```
---
license: other
license_name: calld-teaser
license_link: LICENSE
tags:
  - prediction-markets
  - polymarket
  - uma
  - oracle
  - finance
  - backtesting
  - calibration
pretty_name: Polymarket Resolution Archive — Teaser
size_categories:
  - n<1K
---

# Polymarket Resolution Archive — Teaser Slice

Ground truth for prediction-market research: resolved markets with winning outcomes,
joined to their on-chain UMA Optimistic Oracle settlement records (proposer, disputes,
bonds, settlement lags, tx hashes) and daily price history.

This teaser is the top 500 resolved markets by lifetime volume — a
fully-representative slice of the complete archive (509,357 resolved markets,
3,424 on-chain settlement records, 1,529,475 daily price rows, coverage
January 2022 through 2026-07-22).

## Files

- `markets.csv` — metadata, outcomes, **winner**, final prices, volume, tags
- `provenance.csv` — UMA settlement records: proposer, disputes, bonds, lags, settlement tx
- `prices_daily.csv.gz` — daily prices, long format, per outcome token
- `sample-analysis.ipynb` — calibration curve + settlement-lag analysis, runs as-is
- `DATA-DICTIONARY.md` — column-level docs

## Schema in 10 seconds

`markets.condition_id ↔ prices_daily.condition_id` · `markets.id ↔ provenance.market_id`

Prices are probabilities in [0,1], one row per token-day, UTC. Loads with plain
`pandas.read_csv`.

'''python
import pandas as pd

markets = pd.read_csv("markets.csv")
prices = pd.read_csv("prices_daily.csv.gz", compression="gzip")
prov = pd.read_csv("provenance.csv")

df = prices.merge(markets, on="condition_id")
'''

## What this is (and isn't)

This is the **resolution layer**: outcome labels + on-chain settlement provenance.
It is deliberately daily-granularity — for raw bulk trades or intraday microstructure,
see the excellent free alternatives; this dataset ships the layer they don't.

Scope, stated plainly: every resolved market with ≥$1,000 lifetime volume that traded
```

</details>

## TL;DR

We provide **163GB of historical on-chain trading data** from Polymarket, containing **1.9 billion records** across 538K+ markets. The dataset is directly fetched from Polygon blockchain, fully verified, and ready for analysis. Perfect for market research, behavioral studies, data science projects, and academic research.

## Highlights

- **Complete CLOB Trading History**: Every `OrderFilled` event from Polymarket's two CLOB exchange contracts, with no missing blocks or gaps, from the CLOB launch (first record 2022-11-21 UTC) onward. Note: this covers the on-chain CLOB era only — earlier FPMM/AMM-era trades (2020–Nov 2022), which predate these contracts, are not included.

- **Multiple Analysis Perspectives**: 5 structured datasets at different abstraction levels — raw blockchain events, processed trades with market linkage, market metadata, and derived quantitative views — serving diverse research needs.

- **Production Ready**: Clean, validated data with proper schema documentation. All trades are verified against blockchain RPC, with market metadata linked and ready to use.

- **Open Source Pipeline**: Fully reproducible data collection process. Our open-source tools allow you to verify, update, or extend the dataset independently.

## Dataset Overview

| File | Size | Records | Description |

---
pretty_name: Polymarket Public Trade History
---

# Polymarket Public Trade History

An independent archive of public Polymarket market trade data retrieved from Polymarket Gamma and Data API endpoints. This dataset is not affiliated with or endorsed by Polymarket.

Each `polymarket_raw_N.jsonl` file is an immutable numbered shard. Records contain an asset, event ID, market slug, market window, reported volume, and the public trades returned for that market. Trade fields include side, size, price, timestamp, outcome, outcome index, condition ID, and asset ID. The archive contains no account credentials, private orders, portfolio data, proxy-wallet addresses, usernames, biographies, profile images, or transaction hashes.

Data users are responsible for complying with the [Polymarket API documentation](https://docs.polymarket.com/api-reference/introduction) and other applicable terms.
```

</details>

# Polymarket Resolved Events — Crowd-Belief & Volume Trajectories

**~29,600 fully-resolved Polymarket events, each with its complete daily crowd-belief
probability trajectory, daily trading volume, and ground-truth outcome — crawled directly
from the Polymarket APIs.**

The data is provided as-is from the crawl: every event whose full tradeable lifetime falls
inside the collection window is included, with no quality/liquidity/signal selection
applied. You can apply your own filtering downstream.

## What the dataset contains

- **One file, `full.jsonl`** — JSON Lines, one event per line (~29,600 lines).
- Every event is **resolved** (`ground_truth_status == "resolved"`): the outcome is known,
  so it can be used for supervised forecasting and backtesting.
- Each record bundles four things for one event:
  1. **Event metadata** — title, description, dates, tags, volume.
  2. **Per-option markets** — the underlying binary YES/NO books (1 for a binary event,
     K for a multi-outcome event).
  3. **Daily crowd-belief trajectory** — the market-implied probability for each day of
     the event's tradeable life.
  4. **Daily trading volume** — trades, share volume, and notional per day, aligned to
     the same daily grid.

### Coverage
- **Time window:** events whose entire tradeable lifetime — market open (`start_date`)
  through on-chain resolution (`closed_time`) — falls inside `[2025-06-01, 2026-06-01)` (UTC).
- **Event types:**
  - `binary` — a single YES/NO market.
  - `multi_neg_risk` — a multi-outcome event modeled as K mutually-exclusive, linked
    binary YES/NO books ("neg-risk").
- **Domains:** primarily sports, games, and weather (soccer, cricket, daily temperature),
  plus politics, finance, and others. See each record's `tags`. The domain mix reflects
  Polymarket's activity and is not balanced.

## How it was collected

Crawled from the public Polymarket APIs in four stages:

```

</details>

# Polymarket Full Market Dataset

A **complete, point-in-time archive of Polymarket**: every event and market served by
Polymarket's public Gamma API — from the platform's launch in 2020 through the snapshot
date — with final resolution outcomes, full metadata (questions, rules, tags, timing,
order-book flags), and **dense daily OHLC price candles for every outcome token**,
assembled from the CLOB price-history API.

One snapshot = one self-contained JSON file. One row = one market, with its event
context, resolution, and complete daily price series embedded.

## At a glance (snapshot `2026_07_12_235011`)

| | |
|---|---|
| Market rows | **1,788,703** |
| Distinct events | 679,884 |
| Markets with candle series | **1,787,257 (99.92%)** |
| Embedded daily candle rows | 38,263,931 |
| Markets with a derived resolution | 1,715,580 (95.9%) |
| Time span | 2020-10-02 → 2026-07-12 (snapshot date) |
| File size | 44.9 GB (uncompressed JSON) |
| Schema | `polymarket_market_export_v2` |

Every snapshot ships with a `.summary.json` (same header metadata + one example row)
and machine-readable coverage reports (`polymarket_daily_candles_status.json`, etc.).

![Snapshot analytics: markets per month, category mix, volume distribution, per-cohort coverage](assets/analytics_overview.png)

**Reading the four panels:** (1) market creation exploded in 2026 — driven by
high-frequency recurring series (hourly/5-minute crypto up-or-down, sports); the
peak month alone added 336K markets. (2) Those series dominate the category mix
("Up or Down" + Sports + Esports + Tennis ≈ 75% of all markets). (3) Volume is
bimodal: ~484K markets never traded ($0), while the traded bulk clusters at
$100–$10K lifetime volume and 65 markets exceed $100M. (4) Coverage by creation
cohort — this panel counts **real trade prices** (non-null closes), a stricter
bar than having a candle series (99.9% of markets have one): pre-2023 cohorts
have few real prices because CLOB price history doesn't exist for that era
(structural, not a collection gap), and the 60–85% band in 2025–26 cohorts
reflects ultra-short markets (5-minute/hourly series) whose lifetime is below
the daily price-history fidelity. The resolution-rate drop in the newest cohort
is simply markets that are still open.

## How this snapshot was verified

```

```
---
license: cc-by-nc-4.0
tags:
  - prediction-markets

pretty_name: MLB Cross-Venue Matched Book (Polymarket × Kalshi) — Sample
